TL;DR: ClinHallu是一个用于医学多模态大语言模型阶段性幻觉诊断的基准，包含7,031个验证实例，每个实例都有结构化推理轨迹（视觉识别、知识回忆、推理整合），并通过阶段替换干预和轨迹监督微调来诊断和缓解推理阶段的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学幻觉基准主要关注数据收集，忽视了幻觉在推理过程中源自何处；不同样本的幻觉来源不同，可能来自视觉误识别、错误医学知识回忆或推理整合缺陷，需要细粒度的阶段级诊断工具。

Method: 构建包含7,031个验证实例的基准ClinHallu，每个实例增广为结构化推理轨迹（视觉识别、知识回忆、推理整合）；采用阶段替换干预方法测量纠正特定阶段对最终答案的影响；并通过轨迹监督微调减少阶段性幻觉。

Result: ClinHallu提供了可公开获取的基准，能够进行细粒度的阶段级幻觉诊断；阶段替换干预可量化各阶段对最终答案的影响；轨迹监督微调能有效减少阶段性幻觉。

Conclusion: ClinHallu为医学MLLM中的推理失败诊断与缓解提供了一个细粒度的幻觉测试平台，有助于构建更可信的临床决策支持系统。

Abstract: Building trustworthy medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is critical for reliable clinical decision support. Existing medical hallucination benchmarks mainly focus on data collection, but often ignore where hallucinations originate within the reasoning process. We find that hallucination sources vary across samples: errors may arise from visual misrecognition, incorrect medical knowledge recall, or flawed reasoning integration. To enable source-level hallucination diagnosis, we introduce ClinHallu, a benchmark for stage-wise hallucination diagnosis in medical MLLM reasoning. ClinHallu contains 7,031 validated instances, where each instance is augmented with a structured reasoning trace decomposed into Visual Recognition, Knowledge Recall, and Reasoning Integration. We also use stage-replacement interventions to measure how correcting specific stages affects the final answer. Beyond evaluation, we show that trace-supervised fine-tuning reduces stage-wise hallucinations. ClinHallu provides a fine-grained hallucination testbed for diagnosing and mitigating reasoning failures in medical MLLMs. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu.

</details>


### [107] [Instruct-Particulate: Scaling Feed-Forward 3D Object Articulation with Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14699)
*Ruining Li,Yuxin Yao,Matt Zhou,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Joan Lasenby,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Instruct-Particulate模型，通过引入运动学规范（部件描述、连接关系、关节类型等）来指导3D网格的关节结构重建，利用异构数据集（15万+样本）和大规模视觉语言模型实现跨类别泛化，在AI生成网格和真实图像重建中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络在3D物体关节结构估计上泛化能力有限，主要受限于人工标注数据稀缺。需要一种能够利用异构训练数据并兼容不同粒度标注的方法。

Method: 提出Instruct-Particulate模型，输入3D网格和目标运动学规范（部件描述、连接关系、关节类型及可选点提示），预测部件分割和关节运动参数。训练时利用视觉语言模型构建超过15万个3D物体的异构数据集。

Result: 模型在跨类别泛化和AI生成网格上表现更好，并能通过图像到3D模型实现真实世界图像的关节资产重建。

Conclusion: 引入运动学规范作为条件输入有效解决了数据稀缺和异构性问题，结合大规模视觉语言模型实现了更好的泛化能力，为关节3D物体重建提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing articulated 3D objects is important for animation, gaming, and robotic simulations. Recent neural networks can estimate the articulated structure of 3D objects, but their generalization remains limited by the scarcity of annotated data for this task. To address this gap, we introduce Instruct-Particulate, a model that takes a 3D mesh together with a target kinematic specification, including part descriptions, connectivity, joint types, and optional point prompts, and predicts the corresponding kinematic part segmentation and joint motion parameters. The kinematic specification disambiguates the task and allows the model to target annotations of different granularity, thereby making it possible to use more abundant heterogeneous training data. At test time, the kinematic specification can be obtained automatically from large-scale vision-language models, so the model can be applied to any input mesh. To train our model at scale, we construct a heterogeneous dataset of more than 150,000 articulated 3D objects, extending existing publicly available collections with data obtained by partially labelling other 3D models (monolithic or already decomposed into parts) with kinematic labels by means of vision-language models. Experiments show that our model generalizes better across categories and to AI-generated meshes, enabling articulated asset reconstruction from real-world images via image-to-3D models.

</details>


### [108] [RepFusion: Leveraging Multimodal Priors for Denoising in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14700)
*Xichen Pan,Aashu Singh,Satya Narayan Shukla,Xiangjun Fan,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepFusion利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为噪声表示编码器，替代传统T2I系统中仅将LLM用于文本编码、另训练生成骨干网络的做法，通过MLLM输出的条件信号引导扩散Transformer去噪，在同等推理预算下取得了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I系统中LLM仅用于文本编码，去噪过程由新训练的生成骨干网络处理，未能充分利用LLM的先验知识。受MLLM中MLP投影器足以对齐干净视觉表示与预训练LLM的启发，作者希望将这一机制从干净输入扩展到噪声输入，使MLLM直接参与去噪过程。

Method: RepFusion将MLLM改造为噪声表示编码器，直接用其输出作为扩散Transformer的条件信号。在推理时对不断变化的噪声表示进行重复MLLM条件处理，将测试时的计算资源投入到MLLM推理中。

Result: 在同等推理预算的受控对比中，RepFusion优于将类似容量分配给新初始化解码器的基线方法。

Conclusion: MLLM为去噪视觉表示提供了强大的先验知识，通过在噪声表示上演化地施加MLLM条件，可以在现代T2I系统中有效利用测试时计算资源，证明了将表示自编码器与MLLM结合是一条有前景的技术路线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in text-to-image (T2I) systems, but they are typically limited to text encoding, while denoising is handled by newly trained generative backbones. The emergence of representation autoencoders (RAEs) shifts the generation target toward semantically structured visual representations, creating a latent space that is more compatible with pretrained LLM priors. Inspired by multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), where an MLP projector is sufficient to align clean visual representations with a pretrained LLM, we repurpose the MLLM itself as a noisy representation encoder, extending this mechanism from clean to noisy inputs. We present RepFusion, which uses the resulting MLLM outputs as the conditioning signal for a diffusion transformer. In controlled comparisons at similar inference budgets, RepFusion outperforms baselines that devote comparable capacity to newly initialized denoisers. These results demonstrate that MLLMs provide strong priors for denoising visual representations and that, by conditioning on evolving noisy representations, test-time compute can be productively spent on repeated MLLM conditioning in modern T2I systems.

</details>


### [109] [RATS! Patches Talk Through Registers: Emergent Parts in Register Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14701)
*Timing Yang,Predrag Neskovic,Jansen Seheult,Wenchao Han,Anand Bhattad,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RATS（Register Attention Transformers），通过将分类token分解为多个可学习的register tokens，并利用L->N->N->L瓶颈通信机制，在无需监督信号的情况下，使每个register自发地专门化为类似物体部件的原语义区域，在多个分割基准上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉能够识别物体的可复用部件结构（如鸟的头、翅膀、爪子），而当前的自我监督视觉模型尚未能自主发现这种组合结构，本文旨在探索是否可以通过自监督方式让模型自发学习到这种部件级的组合表示。

Method: 将分类token分解为N个可学习的register tokens，设计三步压缩-通信-广播注意力机制（L->N->N->L瓶颈）；将N个registers分配到H个注意力头上，不同头的registers互不交互；模型通过纯自监督方式训练，不使用辅助损失或部件标注。

Result: 在五个分割基准上平均超越基线方法+12 mIoU；ADE20K上+1.11 mIoU，COCO上+0.2 AP^m；register字典展现跨类别的部件级一致性和语义邻近性。

Conclusion: RATS为结构化、可解释的视觉表示学习提供了一种有用的架构先验，证明了通过简单架构设计即可在自监督条件下自发学习到物体部件级组合结构。

Abstract: When humans see a bird, they recognize far more than just "bird" -- they see a head, wings, and talons, a structured assembly of reusable parts that can be identified across every bird they have ever seen. We ask whether a self-supervised visual model can discover the same compositional structure on its own. To this end, we propose RATS (Register Attention Transformers), which decomposes the classification token into N learnable register tokens that route patch information through an L->N->N->L bottleneck via a three-step compress-communicate-broadcast attention. The N registers are partitioned across the H attention heads, so that registers assigned to different heads do not interact with each other. Without auxiliary losses or part annotations, each register spontaneously specializes into a proto-semantic region whose emerging structure resembles object parts. RATS surpasses all baselines by +12 mIoU on average across five segmentation benchmarks, with consistent gains on ADE20K (+1.11 mIoU) and COCO (+0.2 AP^m). Its register dictionary further exhibits part-level consistency and semantic proximity across related categories. Our results suggest that RATS may provide a useful architectural prior for structured and interpretable visual representation learning.

</details>


### [110] [OmniVideo-100K: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Reasoning through Structured Scripts and Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14702)
*Xinyue Cai,Chaoyou Fu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Ran He,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniVideo自动化数据引擎，通过实体锚定视频脚本和线索引导QA生成两个机制，构建指令微调数据集OmniVideo-100K，解决音视频问答中跨模态关联断裂和长时序推理不足的问题，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升（最高20.59%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频问答管道采用"视频-字幕-QA"范式，存在两个关键问题：(1) 解耦处理音频和视觉模态，破坏了声音与其视觉源之间的内在关联；(2) 对视频进行独立片段处理，导致同一实体在不同片段中的描述不一致；(3) 将长文本理解与QA合成耦合在单一步骤中，限制了模型对局部事件的关注，生成的问题缺乏长程时间关联和深度跨模态推理。

Method: 提出自动化数据引擎，包含两个核心机制：(1) **实体锚定视频脚本**——将视频转化为结构化脚本，包含摘要、主实体列表和逐片段音视频描述，实体列表作为全局先验确保跨片段指代一致性并重构音视频关联；(2) **线索引导QA生成**——先提示模型从脚本中挖掘跨片段、多模态线索，再基于这些高价值线索生成QA对。基于此管道构建了指令微调数据集OmniVideo-100K和人工验证测试集OmniVideo-Test。

Result: 在VITA-1.5、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和Qwen3-Omni-30B上微调，OmniVideo-Test性能提升最高达20.59%；在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等已建立基准上表现强泛化能力，最高提升12.64%。

Conclusion: 提出的自动化数据引擎有效解决了现有音视频问答中的模态关联断裂和长时序推理不足问题，通过结构化脚本和线索引导机制显著提升了多模态QA性能，且具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Current automated pipelines for audio-visual Question Answering (QA) generally adopt a ``video-caption-QA'' paradigm. However, these methods typically segment videos into short clips and generate separate descriptions for audio and visual modalities. This decoupled processing severs inherent associations between sounds and their visual sources, while independent clip processing often causes inconsistent descriptions of the same entity across segments. Furthermore, coupling long-text comprehension and QA synthesis into a single step often restricts models to localized events, yielding questions lacking long-term temporal connections and deep cross-modal reasoning. To address these issues, we propose an automated data engine featuring two mechanisms: (1) \textbf{Entity-Anchored Video Scripting} transforms videos into structured scripts, comprising summaries, main entity lists, and segment-wise audio-visual descriptions. The entity list serves as a global prior to ensure cross-segment referential consistency and reconstruct audio-visual associations. (2) \textbf{Clue-Guided QA Generation} prompts models to first mine cross-segment, multimodal clues from the script, and subsequently generate QA pairs based on these high-value clues. Leveraging this pipeline, we construct the instruction-tuning dataset \textbf{OmniVideo-100K} and a human-verified test set, \textbf{OmniVideo-Test}. Fine-tuning VITA-1.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and Qwen3-Omni-30B on OmniVideo-100K yields performance gains of up to 20.59% on OmniVideo-Test, demonstrating strong generalization (up to 12.64% improvements) across established benchmarks like Daily-Omni and JointAVBench.

</details>


### [111] [Gaze Heads: How VLMs Look at What They Describe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14703)
*Rohit Gandikota,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）的语言骨干中存在一小部分"凝视注意力头"（gaze heads），其注意力会追踪模型当前正在描述的图像区域。通过干预这些头部（不到9%），可以以83.1%的准确率将模型回答重定向到任意选定的漫画面板，甚至可在生成过程中连续切换目标，且该机制跨模型规模（2B-32B）和架构通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLM内部如何解决图像描述任务，找到可解释的、可操作的内部机制，从而无需重新训练即可在推理时控制模型行为。

Method: 1) 以连环漫画为受控测试平台（叙事顺序空间排列），通过少量前向传播计算相关性分数来发现凝视头；2) 对顶部100个凝视头（<9%总头数）应用注意力掩码干预，将注意力重定向到目标区域；3) 在自然图像COCO数据集上验证，并跨不同模型规模和架构进行测试。

Result: 1) 干预顶部100个凝视头以83.1%准确率将回答重定向到任意漫画面板，随机头部干预无效，干预所有头则破坏生成；2) 生成过程中切换凝视目标可在几个token内完成当前面板描述并转向新面板；3) 该机制同样适用于COCO自然图像；4) 跨2B-32B参数规模和多种VLM架构通用，但某些冻结编码器家族无对应头集。

Conclusion: 通过机制分析识别的针对性编辑可作为实用的推理时杠杆来引导多模态模型行为，无需重新训练，为模型可解释性和可控性提供了新方法。

Abstract: How a vision-language model internally solves the task of describing an image is far from obvious. We find that the model develops a specific mechanism for this: a small set of attention heads in its language-model backbone, which we call gaze heads, whose attention tracks the image region the model is currently describing. We find them with a simple correlation score from a few forward passes, using comic strips as a controlled testbed where narrative order is laid out spatially. These gaze heads do not just track the image tokens being described: redirecting their attention to a chosen region forces the VLM to describe that region instead. A single attention-mask intervention on the top-100 gaze heads, fewer than 9% of all heads, steers the model's answer to any chosen comic panel at 83.1% accuracy, while the same intervention on random heads fails to redirect the answer, and intervening on all heads destroys generation. The same lever also extends to continuous control: switching the gaze target mid-generation makes the model wrap up its current panel description and move to the new one within a few tokens. Beyond comics, the same intervention redirects answers to chosen regions in natural COCO images. The mechanism further recurs across model sizes from 2B to 32B parameters and across other VLM architectures, although some frozen-encoder families show no comparable head set. More broadly, this shows that targeted edits identified through mechanistic analysis can serve as practical inference-time levers for steering multimodal model behavior, without any retraining. Our code, interactive demo, and datasets are available at https://gaze.baulab.info/

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [FreoStream:Enhancing Stream Guardrails via Future-Aware Reasoning and Safety-Aligned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13737)
*Jianwei Wang,Guoyang Shen,Yanhong Wu,Haoran Li,Hao Peng,Huiping Zhuang,Cen Chen,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FreoStream是一个新型流式护栏框架，通过未来感知推理（Future-Reason-Judge范式）和安全对齐优化，在保持流式检测的同时，降低了过度拒绝率并提升了越狱防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式护栏存在两个核心问题：(1) 过度拒绝——缺乏完整上下文导致误判安全令牌为不安全；(2) 对隐式越狱内容的检测能力不足。亟需一种既能减少过度拒绝又能增强有害内容检测的流式安全机制。

Method: FreoStream包含两个核心模块：(1) 未来感知推理模块（Future-Aware Reasoning）——当基础护栏检测到不安全令牌时，通过LoRA微调实现未来预测、上下文推理、最终判断的Future-Reason-Judge范式；(2) 安全对齐优化模块（Safety-Aligned Optimization）——从推理梯度中提取安全对齐成分，用于更新基础护栏模型。

Result: 在多个安全基准测试上，FreoStream相比现有流式护栏，实现了更低的过度拒绝率和更好的越狱防御效果。

Conclusion: FreoStream通过将未来信息融入推理过程并结合安全对齐优化，有效解决了流式护栏的过度拒绝和越狱检测两大挑战，为流式安全检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Stream guardrails enable token-level safety detection before full responses are generated. However, they often make overly conservative judgements and block those sensitive but safe tokens, which is known as over-refusal. Due to lack of full context, they also fail to detect implicitly harmful content from jailbreaking. To address these challenges, we propose FreoStream, a novel streaming guardrail framework. Specifically, FreoStream fine-tunes a LoRA module to perform Future-Aware Reasoning when the base guardrail detects unsafe tokens. The reasoning process follows a Future-Reason-Judge paradigm: predict the future, reason about the full context and give the final judgement. This design can effectively reduce over-refusal by incorporating the future information. Moreover, we introduce the Safety-Aligned Optimization module that extracts the safety-aligned component from the reasoning gradients to update the base guardrail model, thereby enhancing streaming safety detection. Extensive experiments on various safety benchmarks demonstrate that FreoStream achieves lower over-refusal rates and better jailbreak defense compared to existing streaming guardrails.

</details>


### [113] [SEVRA-BENCH: Social Engineering of Vulnerabilities in Review Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13757)
*Rui Melo,Riccardo Fogliato,Sean Zhou,Pratiksha Thaker,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SEVRA-BENCH是一个评估LLM代码审查代理能否抵御社会工程攻击的基准测试。它通过将真实CVE漏洞修复反转并包装15种社会工程框架，构建了1,062个恶意PR。评估8个LLM代理发现，闭源与开源模型在安全能力上存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如静态漏洞检测、代码生成）无法评估自动审查代理在面对攻击者同时控制代码变更和PR文本的社会工程攻击时的安全性。作者旨在量化这一风险，并推动开源模型缩小与闭源模型的安全差距。

Method: 从真实项目CVE修复commit中提取漏洞代码反转为恶意PR，附加15种社会工程框架（如虚假声明、虚假证据、紧急性、先验批准、诉诸权威）。基于2025 CWE Top 25中的前10类漏洞，构建1,062个样本。使用8个LLM作为代码审查代理进行测试。

Result: 闭源模型与开源模型在代码审查代理场景下抵御社会工程攻击的能力存在显著差距。

Conclusion: SEVRA-BENCH揭示了当前LLM审查代理的安全漏洞，并可作为推动开源模型安全能力提升的基准资源，以缩小与闭源模型之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) reviewers are increasingly used in pull-request (PR) workflows, where their approvals help decide which code is merged into a repository. This raises a question that benchmarks for static vulnerability detection or code generation do not address: can an automated reviewer reject a malicious contribution when the attacker controls both the code change and the accompanying PR text? We introduce SEVRA-BENCH (Social Engineering of Vulnerabilities in Review Agents), a benchmark that measures how often an automated reviewer approves such adversarial pull requests. Each malicious PR in SEVRA-BENCH is built from a real project commit that previously fixed a vulnerability listed in the Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database. We automatically invert that fix to restore the original vulnerable code and submit it as a pull request wrapped in one of 15 social-engineering framings, which vary the claims made, the supporting evidence, the urgency conveyed, signals of prior approval, and appeals to authority. SEVRA-BENCH contains 1,062 malicious PRs drawn from Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE)-linked fixes across the top 10 entries of the 2025 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) Top 25. In a realistic setting, we evaluate 8 current LLMs as code review agents on PRs that introduce vulnerabilities previously reported in public disclosures. Our results reveal a sharp gap in security capabilities between closed- and open-source models. We hope SEVRA-BENCH will serve as a valuable resource for advancing open-source models and narrowing this gap.

</details>


### [114] [Smart Blockchain-Based Access Control for the Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13798)
*Mahdi Manavi,Yunpeng Zhang,Guoning Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种面向Hyperledger Fabric的风险自适应访问控制层，通过离链LSTM风险预测引擎与链上确定性校验相结合，为不同风险等级的请求分配差异化的背书策略，在不牺牲低延迟的前提下增强高风险的验证强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模物联网部署中，静态背书策略对所有请求施加相同的验证深度，无法根据安全态势动态调整，导致高安全需求场景验证不足或良性流量延迟增加。

Method: 构建离链LSTM风险预测引擎，为每个请求分配风险等级（低/中/高）并签发签名证明；在链码中验证该证明并执行基于风险等级的SBE策略，不修改排序服务或共识机制。

Result: 在Fabric测试平台上，基于风险等级的背书策略增强了高风险请求的验证强度，同时保持了良性工作负载的低确认延迟。

Conclusion: 风险自适应分层验证机制能够在权限区块链中实现安全性与性能的灵活权衡，适用于IoT等大规模部署场景。

Abstract: Securing access control in large-scale Internet of Things (IoT) deployments requires mechanisms that adapt to risk while preserving low latency for benign traffic. Permissioned blockchains such as Hyperledger Fabric offer auditability through smart contracts, but static endorsement policies impose the same validation depth on all requests, regardless of security posture. We propose a risk-adaptive enforcement layer for Hyperledger Fabric that couples an off-chain LSTM-based risk oracle with deterministic on-chain checks. The oracle assigns each request to a tier (Low, Moderate, High) and issues a signed attestation bound to the client identity and target key/version. Endorsing peers verify the attestation in chaincode and enforce tier-conditioned SBE policies without modifying the ordering service or consensus. Experiments on a Fabric testbed show that tier-conditioned endorsement strengthens validation for higher-risk requests while retaining low confirmation latency for benign workloads.

</details>


### [115] [Information Flow Paths from RTL Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13860)
*Calvin Deutschbein,Owyn Wyatt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从RTL轨迹数据中构建信息流路径的新方法，以自动生成并验证安全属性，克服了传统RTL信息流追踪仅能发现寄存器间信息流动而无法重现完整传播路径的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件设计中的安全验证高度依赖人工定义威胁模型、指定安全属性、检测违规及评估后果，流程繁琐且易出错。现有信息流追踪技术仅能检测寄存器间的信息流动，无法捕获敏感信息在设计中的完整传播路径，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 结合信息流追踪与规约挖掘技术，利用新型硬件分析加速技术，提出从RTL轨迹数据中构建信息流路径的新方法，实现敏感信息传播路径的完整复现。

Result: 新方法能够从RTL轨迹数据中成功重现信息流的完整传播路径，突破了先前工作仅能检测寄存器间信息流动的局限。

Conclusion: 该方法为硬件安全验证提供了一种自动化的属性生成与验证途径，有望减少人工干预和人为错误，提升硬件设计安全验证的效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Security validation is an important yet challenging part of the hardware design process, yet, by convention, validation engineers are tasked with defining the threat model, specifying the relevant security properties, detecting any violations of those properties, and assessing the consequences to system security, each of which is manually intensive and may introduce errors. The combined technologies of information flow tracking and specification mining represent an automated approach to property generation and validation, but prior work on information flow tracking on RTL trace data was limited to find cases under which information flowed between registers, without reproducing full paths to capture how sensitive information propagates through a design. With the introduction of new technologies accelerating hardware analysis, we develop a novel approach for constructing information flow paths from register transfer level (RTL) trace data.

</details>


### [116] [RTL-Arrow: Hardware-to-Cloud Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13865)
*Calvin Deutschbein,Jimmy Ostler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Williamette大学硬件安全研究组提出RTL-Arrow框架，将硬件仿真输出的VCD文件转换为云就绪的DataFrame格式，以标准化硬件与软件层面的程序验证流程，减少研究人员手动解析硬件执行轨迹的负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员在识别硬件、软件或接口层的安全漏洞时，需要花费大量时间学习理解和手动解析硬件设计的执行轨迹（trace-of-execution），效率低下且容易出错。

Method: 提出RTL-Arrow框架，将模拟硬件执行的输出（Value Change Dumps, VCD）转换为现代数据科学生成流程中的云就绪DataFrame格式，从而桥接硬件/数据之间的鸿沟，并编译出高性能的二进制程序。

Result: 标准化了硬件与软件层面的程序验证流程；生成了可直接用于数据科学工作流的云兼容数据格式。

Conclusion: RTL-Arrow框架能够有效减少研究人员在硬件安全分析中的手动解析工作，提升从硬件仿真数据到可分析数据格式的转换效率；研究组分享了框架的开发和分发过程中的经验教训。

Abstract: Hardware Security at Willamette is a Willamette University affiliated research group studying the hardware-software interface of security critical services. Within our program, we noticed many researchers spent considerable development time learning to understand and manually parse traces-of-execution of hardware designs which are used to identifying whether vulnerabilities or weaknesses arise at the hardware, software, or interface level. We propose the "RTL-Arrow" framework, a framework to compile performant binaries which bridge the hardware/data divide. We translate the outputs of simulated hardware execution, as "value change dumps" into modern data science workflows as cloud-ready "dataframes", to standardize program verification across the hardware and software levels. We describe our approach, its benefits, and lessons learned from the process of packaging and distributing these libraries for our security research program.

</details>


### [117] [Crypto x AI, AI x Crypto: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13892)
*Sarah Allen,Pranay Anchuri,James Austgen,Maryam Bahrani,Samuel Breckenridge,Aaron Buchwald,Christian Cachin,Andrés Fábrega,Jared Fernandez,James Hsin-yu Chiang,Marwa Mouallem,Roi Bar-Zur,Neil DeSilva,Ittay Eyal,Giulia Fanti,Ari Juels,Andrew Miller,Christian Sillaber,Dani Vilardell,Pramod Viswanath,Wenhao Wang,Matt Weinberg,Sen Yang,Jianzhu Yao,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI与区块链（加密）交叉领域的研究现状，指出两者仍处于早期整合阶段，并梳理了关键机遇、挑战和误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前crypto x AI领域热度高涨但认知混乱，缺乏系统性的梳理来明确已有哪些工作、存在哪些机遇与挑战、以及哪些开放问题值得关注。

Method: 采用综述论文形式，从两个方向（AI对加密技术的贡献、加密技术对AI的贡献）系统化梳理现有工作，总结关键发现，并剖析行业普遍存在的误解。

Result: 系统化了crypto x AI和AI x crypto两个方向的现有研究成果，识别了关键挑战和开放问题，揭示了行业中的常见误解。

Conclusion: AI和加密技术的实质性整合仍处于非常早期的阶段，领域内存在大量待探索的开放问题和需要纠正的误解。

Abstract: The intersection of crypto x AI is spawning papers, products, online posts, and companies. All the surrounding buzz, though, obscures what exactly has been done, what the opportunities and challenges are, and what open questions deserve attention. This survey paper asks what AI can do for blockchain-based technologies (broadly construed as "crypto") (crypto x AI), and vice versa (AI x crypto). We systematize existing work, summarize key takeaways, highlight open research questions, and offer a perspective on pervasive industry misconceptions, concluding that AI and crypto are still in the very early stages of meaningful integration.

</details>


### [118] [Side-Channel Attacks Bypass Protection in 3D Printers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13952)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan,Micah Flack,Gurcan Comert,Judith L. Mwakalonge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次实证评估了商用FDM 3D打印机中部署的主动马达噪声消除（AMNC）对策。AMNC完全中和了声音侧信道（分类准确率接近随机基线8.33%），但未防护的振动信道仍存在泄漏：基于统计特征的粗粒度分类准确率约31%，全序列时序模型可达61%，但该泄漏是设备特定的（跨打印机迁移接近随机），且不足以支持完整的几何重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用FDM 3D打印机已部署AMNC作为针对声音侧信道攻击的硬件防护对策，但此前缺乏对其实际防护效果的实证评估。

Method: 使用两台配备AMNC的Bambu Lab打印机在12种物体类别上的公开同步声音与振动数据集，分别评估声音信道和振动信道的泄漏情况，采用统计特征分类、频率特征分类、全序列时序模型及顺序打乱对照实验。

Result: AMNC完全中和了声音信道（准确率8.33%随机基线）；振动信道存在粗粒度泄漏（统计特征约31%，全序列模型约61%），顺序打乱降至约33%证明序列依赖性；跨打印机迁移准确率接近随机。

Conclusion: AMNC仅为声音防护对策，振动信道仍存在部分几何相关的侧信道泄漏，但不足以支撑完整几何重构；重构级攻击需利用AMNC未防护的磁或功率信道。

Abstract: Active Motor Noise Cancellation (AMNC) ships in commercial fused deposition modeling (FDM) 3D printers as a hardware countermeasure against acoustic side-channel attacks that target intellectual property (IP). We present the first empirical evaluation of a deployed AMNC countermeasure, using a public dataset of synchronized acoustic and vibration recordings from two AMNC-equipped Bambu Lab printers across 12 object classes. AMNC fully neutralizes the acoustic channel: classification accuracy is indistinguishable from the 8.33% random baseline. The vibration channel, which AMNC does not target, still leaks. With summary statistics the leak is coarse and amplitude-driven (vibration accuracy approximately 31% pooled, 36-47% within-printer), while the waveform shape carries essentially nothing (frequency-only features at chance). A full-sequence temporal model that ingests the ordered evolution of the print raises accuracy to approximately 61%, and an order-shuffling control (approximately 33%) shows that a substantial component is genuinely sequential and tied to print progression. The leak is device-specific: a classifier trained on one printer transfers near chance to the other. We conclude that AMNC is an acoustic-only defense: vibration remains a partial, geometry-correlated side channel it does not address, but one that does not, on this dataset, support full geometric reconstruction; reconstruction-grade attacks would require the magnetic or power channels AMNC also leaves untouched. We release all code.

</details>


### [119] [Software Dark Matter: Gazing at Uncharted Files to Navigate SBOM Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13966)
*Abhishek Reddypalle,Dennis Roellke,Santiago Torres-Arias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"软件暗物质"(SDM)概念，指工件文件系统中未被元数据追踪的安全关键文件。通过实现DARKFILES工具分析四个生态系统（DockerHub、Maven Central、插件市场、企业环境），发现SBOM依赖的元数据范式存在根本性缺陷，导致可见性缺口。研究引入工件保真度度量、包装滞后现象，并证明SDM可暴露SBOM管道无法检测的漏洞。发现了三个确认的高严重性CVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件供应链依赖SBOM实现透明度，但当前行业实践基于元数据中心范式，假设工件仅由其包管理器声明定义。这一假设存在根本性缺陷，造成了系统性的可见性缺口，即软件暗物质(SDM)。需要量化并揭示这一安全风险。

Method: 实现参考工具DARKFILES，分析四个异构生态系统：DockerHub、Maven Central、Jenkins插件和OpenVSX插件市场、真实企业环境。引入工件保真度度量（SDM=未追踪文件数/总文件数），定义包装滞后现象，通过交叉引用未追踪包与已知CVE数据库以及直接发现三个确认的高严重性CVE来验证SDM的漏洞暴露能力。

Result: SDM暴露了SBOM驱动管道无法检测的脆弱软件；SDM与敏感信息（包括密钥和加密密钥）高度相关；发现了三个确认的高严重性CVE。

Conclusion: 当前基于元数据的SBOM范式存在根本性缺陷，SDM构成了严重的安全威胁。需要超越元数据依赖，实现对工件文件系统级别的全面透明度。

Abstract: Modern software supply chains have evolved into vast, heterogeneous networks where transparency - the granular understanding of all software components - is now a critical security requirement. While Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) have emerged as the primary mechanism for this transparency, current industry practices rely on a metadata-centric paradigm that assumes an artifact is defined solely by its package manager declarations. We posit that this assumption is fundamentally flawed, creating a systemic visibility gap we define as Software Dark Matter (SDM). SDM represents the set of security-critical files present in an artifact's filesystem that are unaccounted for by its associated metadata. We implement a reference tool, DARKFILES, and use it to analyze four ecosystems of disjoint nature: DockerHub, Maven Central, plugin/extension marketplaces (Jenkins plugins and OpenVSX), and a real-world enterprise environment.
  Our research makes the following contributions: we introduce a general-purpose metric for artifact fidelity calculating SDM as the ratio of untracked files per total file count. We introduce Packaging Lag, a phenomenon where official metadata remains out-of-date across multiple versions before catching up to an artifact's actual content. We demonstrate that SDM exposes vulnerable software invisible to SBOM-driven pipelines both by cross-referencing untracked packages against known CVE databases and through the direct discovery of three confirmed high-severity CVEs, showing that SDM is highly correlated with sensitive information including secrets and cryptographic keys.

</details>


### [120] [Choric Masking in Ambient Release Systems: A Finite Certificate Calculus for Trace Indistinguishability under Bounded Audiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13967)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为环境释放系统构建了一种有限证书演算，通过凸包交集、分离超平面和测度多面体等价性，在有限视界下形式化地认证匿名性、不可链接性和侧信道泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私形式化方法难以处理环境释放系统中保护坐标不可直接观测、但可通过可见角色、时序、重复移动、有限注意力、链接房间和释放后状态统计可读的场景，缺乏可计算的证书机制。

Method: 提出合唱掩蔽(choric masking)安全概念，将轨迹律视为多面体，观众诱导测量算子，掩蔽转化为投影测量空间中的交集；利用分离超平面、核障碍、假设检验界、Fano型定位下界、下游房间支持分离进行暴露认证。

Result: 证明了精确与近似掩蔽的测度-多面体等价性、对偶分离证书、注意力透镜的数据处理律、凝视稀疏观测的光圈恒等式、强制性唯一手势的下界、链接释放的组合规则，以及重复房间债务定理。

Conclusion: 建立了一种有限、可检查的演算语言，用于审计公开面向释放系统中的隐私、不可链接性、侧信道泄漏和问责性。

Abstract: This paper develops a finite certificate calculus for ambient release systems, staged probabilistic environments in which a protected coordinate is not observed directly but can remain statistically readable through visible roles, timing, repeated movement, bounded attention, linked rooms, and post-release state. The security notion, choric masking, requires the trace law induced by a protected locus to lie inside or near the convex hull of admissible cover traces under the tests available to a specified audience. For finite horizons, trace laws form polytopes, audiences induce measurement operators, and masking becomes intersection in the projected measurement space. Exposure is certified by separating hyperplanes, kernel obstructions, hypothesis-testing bounds, Fano-type localization lower bounds, and support separation in downstream rooms. The calculus distinguishes trace residue from carrier localization, full-trace exposure from attention-filtered exposure, first-room masking from delayed post-release exposure, and unresolved system pressure from carrier hazard. It proves measurement-polytope equivalence for exact and approximate masks, dual separation certificates, data-processing laws for attention lenses, aperture identities for gaze-thinned observation, lower bounds for mandatory unique gestures, composition rules for linked releases, and a repeated-room debt theorem showing how unresolved pressure can broaden selection and shift cost onto cover populations without localizing the source. The result is a finite, checkable language for auditing privacy, unlinkability, side-channel leakage, and accountability in public-facing release systems.

</details>


### [121] [Hidden in Plain Sight: Benchmarking Agent Safety Against Decomposition Attacks with DECOMPBENCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13994)
*Vikhyath Kothamasu,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeCompBench基准，专门用于评估LLM智能体在分解攻击下的安全性。实验表明，尽管现有智能体对单一有害任务拒绝率较高，但对分解为看似无害子任务的变体拒绝率显著降低，且常无意中完成恶意目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全基准虽然覆盖多轮、多工具使用场景，但未明确捕捉分解攻击这种将有害任务拆分为良性子任务以绕过安全机制的新型威胁。

Method: 采用"设计即分解"原则，利用图形化框架将有害任务分解为单独良性但可执行的子任务，构建真实的工作流，并设计定制分解器进行实验评估。

Result: 最先进的智能体对单一有害任务拒绝率高，但对分解变体拒绝率显著降低，且常无意中完成对抗性目标。

Conclusion: 需要针对分解攻击的安全评估及相应的防御机制。

Abstract: LLM-based Agents are becoming increasingly capable and widely deployed, creating growing incentives for adversarial misuse in the real-world. A key emerging threat is Decomposition Attacks \cite{glukhov2024breach, jones2024adversaries} in which a harmful task is broken into simpler, benign subtasks that evade safety mechanisms when executed separately but cumulatively fulfill the malicious intent. Although recent benchmarks assess agent safety in multi-turn and multi-tool-use settings, they do not explicitly capture this form of decompositional misuse and may not represent realistic adversarial execution flows. To this end, we introduce DeCompBench, a benchmark designed specifically to evaluate agentic safety under decomposition attacks. DeCompBench is created with a decomposition-by-design principle using a graphical framework and enables harmful task decomposition into individually benign and executable subtasks with realistic workflows. Our experiments using a custom decomposer show that state-of-the-art agents exhibit high refusal rates on monolithic harmful tasks, but significantly lower refusal rates on their decomposed variants, while often inadvertently fulfilling the adversarial objectives. These findings underscore the need for safety evaluations against decomposition attacks and corresponding defenses. Our dataset is publicly available and can be found at https://huggingface.co/datasets/decompositionbench/DeCompBench.

</details>


### [122] [Pseudonym Scheme Based on Hybrid Certificates for Security Credential Management System in Vehicular Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14008)
*Abel C. H. Chen,F. J. Hwang,Yu-Chih Wei,Chin-Chen Chang,Bon-Yeh Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究针对IEEE和ETSI的车载通信安全标准主要基于ECC、可能易受量子计算攻击的问题，提出了一种结合ECC与后量子密码学(PQC)的混合证书方案，并设计了兼容多种密码算法的通用化假名方案，以消除假名证书与注册证书公钥之间的关联性。通过对RSA、ECC和NIST标准化的PQC算法进行性能评估，推荐了适用于车载通信的安全高效混合证书假名方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IEEE和ETSI车载通信安全标准（如SCMS、BKE）主要基于ECC，未来可能面临量子计算攻击的威胁，需要构建抗量子攻击的密码学基础设施。

Method: 提出ECC与PQC结合的混合证书方案；设计兼容多种密码算法的通用化假名机制，消除假名证书与注册证书公钥间的关联性；对RSA、ECC及NIST标准化PQC算法进行消息长度和计算时间等性能评估。

Result: 完成了混合证书方案的设计与通用化假名方案的构建；通过性能对比分析，给出了适用于车载通信的混合证书假名方案推荐。

Conclusion: 该研究为车载通信提供了一种面向量子时代的安全密码学基础设施方案，混合证书能够平衡抗量子攻击能力与性能效率。

Abstract: In recent years, the Institute of Electrical and Electronics Engineers (IEEE) and the European Telecommunications Standards Institute (ETSI) have developed a series of security communication standards for vehicular communications. These standards include mechanisms such as the Security Credential Management System (SCMS) and Butterfly Key Expansion (BKE) to protect vehicle privacy. However, these standards are mainly based on the Elliptic-Curve Cryptography (ECC), which may be vulnerable to attacks from quantum computing in the future. In response to this potential risk, this study proposes a hybrid certificate that combines the ECC with Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). This approach enables infrastructure systems to be built on cryptographic foundations that are more resilient to quantum-based attacks. Furthermore, this study presents a generalized pseudonym scheme that is compatible with various cryptographic algorithms for generating pseudonym certificates. This design aims to eliminate the possibility of inferring any correlation between the public key in a pseudonym certificate and that in an enrollment certificate. This study also conducts a comprehensive performance evaluation of the RSA, ECC, and PQC algorithms, particularly those standardized by the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST). The comparison considers factors such as message length and computation time. Based on the findings, this study recommends suitable pseudonym schemes that adopt hybrid certificates for secure and efficient use in vehicular communications.

</details>


### [123] [Same-Origin Policy for Agentic Browsers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14027)
*Xilong Wang,Xiaoxing Chen,Patrick Li,Dawn Song,Neil Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主AI代理浏览器中同源策略（SOP）的有效性问题，发现现有代理浏览器在良性场景和攻击下均频繁违反SOP。作者构建了SOPBench基准测试，并提出了针对代理浏览器定制的SOP实施机制SOPGuard，在BrowserOS中实现，实验证明其能有效执行SOP、保持实用性且运行时开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同源策略（SOP）是浏览器防止脚本引发未授权跨源数据流的基础安全机制，但自主AI代理浏览器（Agentic Browser）的出现使得SOP的有效性成为悬而未决的问题，目前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 首先观察发现代理浏览器本身可作为跨源数据流的自动化通道，可能导致SOP违反；据此构建了用于评估代理浏览器中SOP违反情况的基准测试SOPBench；提出针对代理浏览器的SOP实施机制SOPGuard，并在开源代理浏览器BrowserOS中实现。

Result: 评估表明现有代理浏览器在良性设置和攻击下均频繁违反SOP；SOPGuard能有效执行SOP，同时保持实用性，仅引入较小的运行时开销。

Conclusion: 代理浏览器存在严重的SOP违反问题，SOPGuard是一种有效的解决方案，可在不牺牲实用性的前提下保障同源策略的执行。

Abstract: Agentic browsers integrate autonomous AI agents into web browsers, enabling users to accomplish web tasks through natural-language instructions. The same-origin policy (SOP) is a fundamental browser security mechanism that prevents unauthorized automated cross-origin data flows induced by scripts. However, whether SOP remains effective in agentic browsers is an open question that has not been systematically studied. In this work, we bridge this gap. We first observe that an agentic browser can itself serve as an automated channel for cross-origin data flows, potentially leading to SOP violations. To investigate this phenomenon, we construct SOPBench, a benchmark for evaluating SOP violations in agentic browsers. Our evaluation shows that existing agentic browsers frequently violate SOP, both in benign settings and under attacks. To address this problem, we propose SOPGuard, an SOP enforcement mechanism tailored to agentic browsers. We implement SOPGuard in BrowserOS, an open-source agentic browser. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SOPGuard effectively enforces SOP while preserving utility and incurring only a small runtime overhead. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/wxl-lxw/BrowserOS-SOPGuard.

</details>


### [124] [Defending the Core: A Centrality-Based Protection Strategy for Supply Chain Security in npm Dependency Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14036)
*Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过分析npm依赖网络（53,481个包和78,520条依赖边），发现其呈无标度拓扑结构，易受针对高中心性枢纽节点的定向攻击。为此提出双重防御策略：基于中心性的节点强化与依赖权重预警系统。模拟证明对前1%节点实施严格安全协议并修剪30%的结构性次要边，可防止网络级联崩溃并阻断恶意软件传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件供应链（如npm依赖网络）严重依赖共享开源依赖，虽促进快速开发但也引入系统性漏洞，需评估其脆弱性并提出防御方案。

Method: 对53,481个包和78,520条依赖边建模，分类为无标度拓扑；提出双重防御策略：基于中心性的节点强化（Centrality-Based Node-Hardening）与依赖权重预警系统（Dependency Weight Warning）；模拟多种攻击场景验证策略效果。

Result: 对前1%节点实施严格安全协议并结合修剪30%结构性次要边，可防止网络级联崩溃并阻断级联恶意软件感染。

Conclusion: npm依赖网络呈现无标度拓扑，对高中心性枢纽节点存在固有脆弱性；所提双重防御策略能有效提升网络韧性，降低供应链攻击风险。

Abstract: The modern software supply chain, taking Node Package Manager (npm) dependency network for example, relies heavily on shared open-source dependencies. While this promotes rapid development, it introduces systemic vulnerabilities as well. Concerning this potential risk, we analyze the npm dependency network by modeling 53,481 packages and 78,520 dependency edges, and classify the network as a scale-free topology. Thus, we demonstrate its inherent vulnerability to targeted attacks on high-degree hubs. To mitigate this, we propose and evaluate a dual-pronged defense strategy consisting of Centrality-Based Node-Hardening and Dependency Weight Warning system. Moreover, by simulating the network under various attack scenarios, we prove that applying strict security protocols to just the top 1% of nodes, combined with pruning 30% of structurally trivial edges, prevents catastrophic network collapse and neutralizes cascading malware infections. The source code can be found at https://github.com/5tarWhee1/Centrality-Based-Protection-Strategy-for-Supply-Chain-Security-in-npm-Dependency-Network.

</details>


### [125] [Hierarchical Identity-Based Signature with Designated Aggregator from Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14090)
*Stuti Kumari,Kunal Dey,Vikas Srivastava,Sumit Kumar Debnath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于格理论的层级身份基签名与指定聚合器（HIBS-DA）方案，允许不同层级用户生成可聚合为单一紧凑签名的签名，降低通信与验证开销，适用于大型层级组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级组织中多用户数据认证复杂且资源密集，现有方案效率不足，需要一种支持签名聚合、降低通信与验证成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出HIBS-DA框架，并基于格理论（lattice-based）构造首个实现方案，支持不同层级用户生成可聚合的签名。

Result: 实现了首个基于格的HIBS-DA构造，签名可被聚合为单一紧凑签名，显著减少通信与验证开销。

Conclusion: 方案具备安全性、正确性及抗伪造性，适用于大学、企业、政府机构等大规模层级环境。

Abstract: In hierarchical organizations, authenticating data from multiple users can be complex and resource-intensive. Hierarchical Identity-Based Signature with Designated Aggregator (HIBS-DA) provides an efficient solution by allowing users at different levels to generate signatures that can be combined into a single, compact signature. We first introduce the HIBS-DA framework and present the {\em{first}} lattice-based construction of HIBS-DA. Our scheme allows users at different hierarchical levels to generate individual signatures that can be aggregated into a single, compact signature, reducing communication and verification costs. The proposed construction is secure, correct, and resistant to forgery, making it suitable for large-scale environments such as universities, corporations, and government agencies.

</details>


### [126] [SkillMutator: Benchmarking and Defending Language-and-Code Cross-modal Attacks on LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14154)
*Youngduk Kim,Minkyoo Song,Seungwon Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMutator基准测试，用于检测LLM Agent技能中的跨模态攻击（自然语言与代码的交互攻击）。现有检测器仅能发现2%-17%的攻击。作者通过四阶段推理轨迹蒸馏框架，将前沿模型的能力蒸馏到7B开源模型上，检测率从17.1%提升至88.2%，超过了GPT-4o-mini并接近GPT-5.4水平，证明无需依赖昂贵模型即可实现有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent技能的标准格式同时包含自然语言指令（SKILL.md）和可执行脚本，攻击者可在SKILL.md中描述良性工作流的同时嵌入隐式恶意指令，诱导Agent泄露敏感文件。这种跨模态攻击面此前未被充分研究，现有技能扫描器仅将其视为提示注入或静态代码问题，无法检测跨模态交互产生的攻击。

Method: 1) 提出SkillMutator基准，模拟对抗性变异过程，覆盖13种攻击类别，利用扫描器反馈迭代优化恶意技能，使其与合法工作流难以区分。2) 提出四阶段推理轨迹蒸馏框架，将前沿教师模型的推理轨迹蒸馏到小规模开源模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct），实现本地可部署的检测器。

Result: 开源扫描器仅检测2%-8%的攻击，商业扫描器仅检测9%-17%。蒸馏后的7B模型在最强SkillMutator子集上检测率从17.1%提升至88.2%，超过GPT-4o-mini（23.7%）和GPT-5.4-mini（79.0%），达到GPT-5.4水平（86.8%）。

Conclusion: 针对跨模态攻击的实用防御是可行的，无需依赖昂贵的前沿模型，通过蒸馏技术即可实现有效的本地化检测。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly extend their capabilities at runtime by loading Agent Skills, which pair natural-language specifications (SKILL.md) with executable scripts and resources. Because a skill's behavior relies on both natural-language instructions and executable code, assessing its safety requires cross-modal reasoning, creating a new language-and-code attack surface. Attackers can present a benign workflow in SKILL.md while embedding implicit directives that steer the agent to exfiltrate sensitive files, even if the scripts appear harmless. This attack surface remains understudied; prior work treats skills merely as prompt-injection vectors or static code artifacts, leaving attacks emerging from cross-modal interactions largely unmeasured. In our evaluation, open-source and commercial skill scanners detect only 2%-8% and 9%-17% of such attacks, respectively. To address this gap, we introduce SkillMutator, the first benchmark for install-time detection of language-and-code cross-modal attacks on Agent Skills. It emulates an adversarial mutation process across 13 attack categories, iteratively refining malicious skills using scanner feedback to make injected behaviors indistinguishable from legitimate workflows. We further propose a four-phase reasoning-trajectory distillation framework to distill frontier-teacher traces into smaller open-weight models. This produces a locally deployable scanner avoiding third-party data exposure and excessive API costs. On the strongest SkillMutator subset (n=76), our distilled model (Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct) improves detection from 17.1% to 88.2%, surpassing GPT-4o-mini (23.7%) and GPT-5.4-mini (79.0%), and reaching frontier-level GPT-5.4 (86.8%). These results show practical defense against cross-modal attacks is feasible without relying on costly frontier models.

</details>


### [127] [Security Evaluation of Mobile Banking Applications in Sudan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14165)
*Abdelmonim Naway*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对苏丹四款主流移动银行应用（Bankak、Fawry、Okash、Sahil，覆盖超160万用户）进行安全审计，使用SAST工具（MobSF、Quixxi）依据OWASP MASVS标准评估，发现Bankak风险最高（12个漏洞），所有应用存在安全随机数生成缺陷（CWE-330），且生产APK均保留调试符号，整体安全态势堪忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苏丹金融数字化快速推进导致移动银行应用激增，但安全审计未能同步跟进，存在严重安全隐患，亟需对主流应用进行系统性技术审计以识别漏洞并推动行业安全改进。

Method: 使用Mobile Security Framework（MobSF）和Quixxi进行静态应用安全测试（SAST），依据OWASP MASVS标准评估，并将发现映射到通用弱点枚举（CWE）标识符进行系统性漏洞分析。

Result: Bankak风险最高（12个漏洞），缺少SSL证书绑定、存在不安全的TrustManager实现；所有应用普遍存在安全随机数生成缺陷（CWE-330）；Bankak和Okash使用MD5/SHA-1等过时加密算法；所有应用禁用ADB备份但生产APK均保留冗长调试符号。

Conclusion: 研究填补了苏丹金融科技生态系统的安全审计空白，提供了面向开发者的技术建议和在全行业实施"安全设计"原则的战略路线图。

Abstract: The rapid digitalization of the Sudanese financial sector has precipitated a surge in Mobile Banking Applications (MBAs); however, this growth has frequently outpaced rigorous security auditing. This study provides a comprehensive technical audit of the four most widely used Sudanese MBAs( Bankak, Fawry, Okash, and Sahil )collectively serving a user base of over 1.6 million. Utilizing Static Application Security Testing (SAST) via the Mobile Security Framework (MobSF) and Quixxi, the applications were evaluated against the OWASP Mobile Application Security Verification Standard (MASVS). Findings were mapped to Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) identifiers to identify systemic vulnerabilities. Analysis revealed critical disparities in security posture. Bankak, the market leader, exhibited the highest risk profile (12 vulnerabilities), including a critical absence of SSL certificate pinning and unsafe TrustManager implementations, rendering it highly susceptible to Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) attacks. While Fawry demonstrated relative maturity (7 vulnerabilities), a universal failure was observed across all four applications regarding secure random number generation (CWE-330), potentially compromising session token integrity. Additionally, Bankak and Okash were found to utilize deprecated cryptographic algorithms (MD5/SHA-1). Notably, all applications successfully disabled ADB backups, yet 100% retained verbose debugging symbols in production APKs, significantly lowering the barrier for reverse engineering. This research addresses a critical gap in the national fintech ecosystem by providing actionable technical recommendations for developers and a strategic roadmap for implementing "security-by-design" principles across the sector.

</details>


### [128] [From Prompts to Responses: Dual-Sided Data Leakage and Defense in Split Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14210)
*Zixuan Gu,Xiaojun Ye,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Split-LLM中针对输入提示和输出响应的双向隐私攻击（PIDI），并提出基于适配器的双向防御方法（ADMI），在保护隐私的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Split-LLM在隐私敏感场景中部署时，现有研究主要关注通过中间表示反演攻击泄露输入提示，而忽略了生成性输出响应中潜在的信息泄露风险。

Method: 提出PIDI（补丁模型反演+双端初始化）两阶段攻击方法，同时攻击私有输入提示和输出响应；提出ADMI（基于适配器的双向防御+互信息正则化）防御方法。

Result: PIDI显著优于以往反演方法；ADMI有效防御PIDI及其他最先进的反演攻击，同时最小化对任务性能的影响。

Conclusion: Split-LLM存在双向隐私泄露漏洞，ADMI能够在保持性能的同时提供强经验隐私保护。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in privacy-sensitive domains, where users must balance the risk of data exposure through external APIs against the high computational cost of local deployment. Split learning has therefore emerged as a promising paradigm for LLM fine-tuning and inference under limited local resources. However, it introduces new privacy risks. Prior work primarily studies leakage of private input prompts, typically via inversion attacks on intermediate representations, while the potential for sensitive information leakage through generative response outputs remains largely unexplored.
  In this work, we unveil novel vulnerabilities of Split-LLM by presenting Patched Model Inversion with Dual-Sided Initialization (PIDI), a two-stage attack that simultaneously targets both private input prompts and output responses in Split-LLM settings. It combines dual-sided initialization with a patched inversion strategy to tackle long sequences, substantially outperforming prior inversion methods. To counter threats from both sides, we further propose the Adapter-based DualGuard with Mutual Information Defense (ADMI), which integrates an adapter-based local warmup strategy and mutual information regularization to provide a strong empirical privacy protection with minimal impact on task performance. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks and models demonstrate that ADMI effectively defends against PIDI and other state-of-the-art inversion attacks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/FLAIR-THU/VFLAIR-LLM.

</details>


### [129] [Security in a Workflow: Exploring Role-Based Agentic Architectures for Vulnerability Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14261)
*Srijita Basu,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角色的智能体工作流（Planner、Analyzer、Fixer、Verifier），用于模拟工业级软件安全漏洞分析与修复流程，在25个真实C/C++漏洞上实现了44%的检测准确率和19%的修复准确率，并探讨了集成CodeQL等静态分析工具的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的软件安全方法多聚焦于检测或补丁生成等孤立任务，缺乏反映工业实际中多阶段工作流的智能体架构，导致现有方法与真实实践之间存在差距。

Method: 设计了一个基于角色的智能体工作流，包含Planner（规划）、Analyzer（分析）、Fixer（修复）、Verifier（验证）四个角色；其中一种工作流中Analyzer集成了CodeQL静态分析工具；使用了nemotron-cascade-2:30b、qwen3-coder-next、gpt-oss:120b等模型，在25个真实C/C++漏洞数据集上评估。

Result: 漏洞检测准确率为44%，修复准确率为19%。

Conclusion: 该角色型智能体工作流初步验证了模拟工业级安全流程的可行性，但修复准确率仍较低，需进一步提升；研究结果对软件安全从业者具有实践启示。

Abstract: Secure software engineering in practice is a multi-stage workflow involving vulnerability analysis, remediation, and fix verification. However, current LLM-based software security approaches often focus on isolated tasks such as detection or patch generation, with limited attention to agentic architectures reflecting industrial workflow. This creates a gap between existing LLM-based vulnerability-handling methods and real-world practices. In this paper, we study a role-based agentic workflow for vulnerability analysis and mitigation consisting of Planner, Analyzer, Fixer, and Verifier roles. To explore the effect of static analysis tool, the analyzer agent was integrated with the CodeQL in one of the workflows. The models used include nemotron-cascade-2:30b, qwen3-coder-next, and gpt-oss:120b. Our evaluation uses 25 real-world C/C++ vulnerabilities. The study reports 44% vulnerability detection accuracy comparable to GPT 5.5 and 19% fix accuracy. We also list implications from this study in context of software security practitioners.

</details>


### [130] [AgentCyberRange: Benchmarking Frontier AI Systems in Realistic Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14295)
*Fengyu Liu,Jiarun Dai,Yihe Fan,Wuyuao Mai,Ziao Li,Bofei Chen,Jie Zhang,Zheng Lou,Bocheng Xiang,Qiyi Zhang,Xudong Pan,Geng Hong,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentCyberRange，首个开源、多靶场的自主网络攻击能力评估基础设施，包含110个漏洞、15个真实Web应用、8个企业级靶场（156个内部主机）及Cage工具链。评估6个前沿AI系统，GPT-5.5+Codex表现最佳（Web利用16.1%，后渗透31.7%），提示增强后分别提升至33.0%和46.3%。还发现了流行项目的未知漏洞和绕过主机防御的载荷变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准（如CTF、漏洞复现、利用生成）仅评估孤立的网络安全技能，缺乏模拟真实入侵流程（服务发现、立足、信息收集、横向移动）的多主机靶场，导致前沿AI系统的真实攻击能力难以在可控条件下被早期观测到。

Method: 构建开放、可复现的多靶场基础设施AgentCyberRange，整合110个漏洞、15个真实Web应用、8个企业级靶场（156个内部主机），并开发Cage工具链用于执行、编排、结果收集和验证。基准测试覆盖Web利用和后渗透两个核心阶段，在匹配的提示和预算下评估6个前沿AI系统。

Result: GPT-5.5+Codex在Web利用任务中解决16.1%，后渗透任务中解决31.7%；提供更具体提示后分别提升至33.0%和46.3%。此外观察到基准之外的发现：流行项目中的未知漏洞、绕过主机防御的载荷变异。

Conclusion: 开放靶场评估对于在真实且可复现条件下观察新兴的自主攻击能力是必要的。AgentCyberRange能够揭示前沿AI系统在现实攻击场景中的能力边界及潜在风险。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems are increasingly capable of cybersecurity tasks, including codebase inspection, vulnerability detection, and exploitation. However, evaluating their offensive capabilities remains constrained by limited access to open, reproducible, multi-host cyber ranges. Existing public benchmarks capture isolated skills such as CTF solving, vulnerability reproduction, and exploit generation, but often abstract away realistic intrusion workflows: discovering exposed services, gaining a foothold, collecting internal information, and expanding compromise across hosts. This gap makes it difficult to observe emerging risks early, because frontier AI systems are rarely evaluated under realistic attack conditions.
  We introduce AgentCyberRange, the first open, multi-range infrastructure for measuring autonomous cyber attack capability in realistic cyber ranges. It combines 110 vulnerabilities across 15 real web applications and 8 enterprise-like cyber ranges with 156 internal hosts, plus Cage, a toolchain for execution, orchestration, result collection, and verification. The benchmark covers two core stages: web exploitation, where agents explore exposed applications and validate vulnerabilities, and post exploitation, where agents turn an initial foothold into broader internal compromise. We evaluate six frontier AI systems under matched prompts and budgets. GPT-5.5 with Codex performs best, solving 16.1% of web exploitation tasks and 31.7% of post-exploitation tasks; with more concrete hints, these rates increase to 33.0% and 46.3%. We also observe out-of-benchmark findings, including unknown vulnerabilities in popular projects, and payload mutation that bypasses host defenses. These results show that open cyber-range evaluation is necessary for observing emerging offensive capabilities under realistic and reproducible conditions.

</details>


### [131] [REPOSE: Quantifying the Price of Security in Weakly-Hard Real-Time Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14395)
*Vijay Banerjee,Monowar Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出REPOSE分析框架，用于在运行时评估弱实时控制系统的安全可行性，通过主动和被动安全操作实现安全与安全的有限权衡，在80%利用率下仅产生0.06%的可行性开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IoT边缘设备安全机制存在"全有或全无"困境：要么安全任务因高利用率被忽略，要么给每个任务添加安全机制导致性能大幅下降。亟需一种能实现安全与安全边界权衡的运行时分析方法。

Method: 提出REPOSE分析框架，针对弱实时控制系统，采用主动（proactive）和被动（reactive）相结合的安全操作方式，基于任务当前行为动态执行安全操作，而非在设计时进行悲观的额外开销分配。

Result: 在80%利用率下，REPOSE的可行性开销仅为0.06%，而硬约束系统为29%。通过经典控制系统的案例研究，证明了REPOSE能够有效分析和计算安全与安全之间的权衡。

Conclusion: REPOSE为实时网络物理系统提供了一种有效的运行时安全可行性分析框架，在保证安全性的同时显著降低了性能开销，实现了安全与安全的可量化权衡。

Abstract: In contemporary IoT edge devices with real-time requirements, security is primarily enforced through design-time parameters associated with security tasks, leading to mechanisms that operate in an \emph{opportunistic} manner. As a result, security checks are often performed as secondary operations. This approach can result in systems where no security tasks are executed due to high utilization by other tasks. An alternative approach taken in prior work is to add security mechanisms to every task in the system, resulting in substantially lower performance than that of a system with no security. These approaches have resulted in an \emph{all-or-nothing} scenario for edge device security, motivating numerous studies on the safety-security trade-off in real-time cyber-physical systems (RT-CPS). This study introduces an analytical framework -- REPOSE -- for evaluating the security feasibility of real-time control systems at runtime. REPOSE is developed for \textit{weakly-hard} real-time control systems that facilitate a ``bounded trade-off'' between safety and security. In contrast to imposing additional (pessimistic) design-time overhead as considered in some real-time security literature, REPOSE performs security operations in both \textit{proactive} and \textit{reactive} manners based on the task's current behavior. Our evaluations show that REPOSE can effectively add security operations to RT-CPS with a feasibility overhead of $0.06\%$ at $80\%$ utilization, compared to a $ 29\%$ overhead observed in systems with hard constraints. Through a case study of a classic control system, we also demonstrate that REPOSE provides a robust framework to \textit{analyze and calculate} the safety-security tradeoff.

</details>


### [132] [Breaking TinyML: Why Quantized Neural Networks Need Domain-Specific Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14427)
*Jacob Huckelberry,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Yuke Zhang,Peter A. Beerel,James Mickens,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对TinyML硬件加速器中量化神经网络安全研究空白，提出了一种两阶段攻击流水线，在量化神经网络背景下超越了现有技术水平，论证了硬件感知安全研究的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TinyML硬件广泛使用量化神经网络以满足功耗和尺寸约束，但量化过程的安全性尚未被充分研究；现有研究指出QNN对典型逃逸攻击具有与全精度DNN相当或更强的鲁棒性，但尚无专门针对TinyML硬件的攻击策略。

Method: 提出一种两阶段攻击流水线（two-step attack pipeline），专门针对TinyML硬件中的量化神经网络设计，实现超越现有最先进方法的攻击效果。

Result: 该两阶段攻击流水线在量化神经网络背景下超越了当前最先进的攻击方法。

Conclusion: TinyML硬件的安全性亟需更多硬件感知的安全研究，量化神经网络的鲁棒性评估需考虑硬件特性。

Abstract: Most TinyML hardware accelerators focus on supporting Quantized Neural Networks (QNNs) to meet stringent constraints on power consumption and size. Despite this, the security aspects of quantization within TinyML hardware remain largely unexplored. Although previous studies indicate that QNNs demonstrate similar or enhanced robustness when compared to full-precision Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) against typical evasion attacks, no attack strategies tailored specifically for TinyML hardware have been proposed yet. This paper addresses this shortfall by demonstrating how a two-step attack pipeline can surpass the current state-of-the-art in the QNN context and shows the need for more hardware-aware security research.

</details>


### [133] [Securing the Future of IoMT in the Post-Quantum Era: An Edge-Native Federated Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14515)
*Taym Alshoghri,Deemah H. Tashman,Mohammad Reza Gerami,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个基于Kubernetes的后量子密码集成框架，用于联邦学习场景下的医疗物联网系统，通过在树莓派测试平台上的实验证明分布式密码处理相比顺序设计能显著降低延迟，同时保持可行的资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网设备资源受限且处理高度敏感的健康数据，联邦学习中的模型更新可能泄露隐私信息，同时量子计算威胁着现有轻量级密码机制的安全性，因此亟需将后量子密码集成到IoMT系统中。

Method: 设计了一个可扩展的基于Kubernetes的框架，将后量子密码集成到联邦学习驱动的IoMT环境中，并在树莓派测试平台上进行了实验验证。

Result: 分布式密码处理相比顺序设计显著降低了延迟，且资源开销在可行范围内。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献在于为联邦学习驱动的IoMT系统设计并验证了一个安全的编排与通信框架，未来方向包括能量感知架构、智能安全优化以及弹性的下一代智能医疗物联网生态系统。

Abstract: Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices operate under strict resource constraints while handling highly sensitive health data, making security and privacy critical concerns. Federated learning (FL) further complicates this landscape, as model updates exchanged during training may unintentionally expose private medical information. Emerging quantum computing capabilities threaten the long-term viability of conventional lightweight cryptographic mechanisms, motivating the integration of Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) into IoMT systems. This article discusses key enabling technologies for quantum-resilient IoMT, including post-quantum key establishment, lightweight encryption, and edge-native orchestration. We propose a scalable Kubernetes-based framework that integrates PQC into FL-enabled IoMT environments and validate it on a Raspberry Pi testbed. Results demonstrate that distributed cryptographic processing significantly reduces latency compared to sequential designs while maintaining feasible resource overhead. The primary contribution of this work lies in the design and validation of a secure orchestration and communication framework for FL-enabled IoMT systems. We conclude by outlining future directions toward energy-aware architectures, intelligent security optimization, and resilient next-generation Intelligent Internet of Medical Things (IIoMT) ecosystems.

</details>


### [134] [From Shield to Target: Denial-of-Service Attacks on LLM-Based Agent Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14517)
*Yuguang Zhou,Xunguang Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Zhantong Xue,Zhaoyu Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于LLM的护栏在防御提示注入和越狱攻击时，因自身推理能力引发的新型拒绝服务（DoS）漏洞。攻击者通过注入精心构造的数据，使护栏陷入长推理循环。作者设计了束搜索优化框架和基于结构变异的攻击方法，在多个模型和真实Agent系统中实现了高达148倍的延迟放大，单个恶意文档即可瘫痪共享护栏基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM护栏有效防御了提示注入和越狱攻击，但其强大的推理和指令遵循能力本身可能被利用，导致计算资源耗尽。目前缺乏对这一可用性漏洞的系统性研究。

Method: 1）束搜索优化框架：利用LLM提案器配合策略库生成自然语言载荷，最大化护栏推理长度。2）基于机制感知结构变异的攻击框架：利用护栏的schema遵循特性，计算负载更低。在独立评估和端到端真实Agent部署（网页、桌面、代码、多Agent系统）中进行系统性评估。

Result: 独立评估中，优化的载荷在8个主流模型（Claude、GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、Qwen等）上实现13-63倍令牌放大；端到端评估中实现高达148倍延迟放大。单个恶意文档即可饱和共享护栏基础设施，瘫痪共址Agent及整个系统。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM护栏在可用性层面的新型安全漏洞，强调了开发成本有界、推理鲁棒的护栏的紧迫性。

Abstract: LLM-based guardrails have emerged as a highly effective defense against prompt injection and jailbreak attacks in autonomous agents. However, we reveal that the very reasoning and task-following capabilities enabling this protection introduce a novel vulnerability: attackers can inject crafted data to trap the guardrail in extended reasoning loops, effectuating a systematic denial-of-service (DoS) attack. To systematically expose this threat, we design a beam-search optimization framework that crafts natural-language payloads to maximize guardrail reasoning length, utilizing an LLM proposer guided by a strategy bank. Based on the observation of guardrail's schema-following nature, we also provide another attack framework driven by mechanism-aware structural mutations with less computational load. The attack efficacy is systematically evaluated in two parts. First, in standalone evaluations, the attack generalizes across diverse guardrail architectures, safety templates, and agent benchmarks. Payloads optimized on a single open-source surrogate successfully transfer to eight leading model backbones (e.g., Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Qwen), achieving a 13--63$\times$ token amplification. Second, in end-to-end real-world agent deployments (web, desktop, code, and multi-agent systems), the attack reveals up to a 148$\times$ latency amplification. We show that a single poisoned document can saturate shared guardrail infrastructures, effectively starving co-located agents and paralyzing the entire system. By uncovering this availability flaw, our work underscores the urgent need to develop cost-bounded, reasoning-robust guardrails.

</details>


### [135] [Detecting Bot Detection: Prevalence, Techniques, and Implications for Web Measurement Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14525)
*Ralf Gundelach,Michael Mühlhauser,Dominik Herrmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对10,000个网站的测量研究，发现83%的顶会论文未讨论机器人检测导致的样本损失问题；Chromium headless模式面临15%的软拦截率，其中82%的拦截可归因于机器人检测（以Cloudflare和Akamai为主），75%的headless-only拦截由头部信号引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注机器人检测的部署情况，但缺乏对机器人检测导致的样本丢失（blocking-induced sample loss）的系统量化；而网络测量研究的有效性正因自动化浏览器可能被拦截或提供不同内容而受到威胁。

Method: 1）对顶级安全、隐私和网络测量会议的文献调查，评估论文对机器人检测拦截的讨论情况；2）对10,000个网站在四种浏览器配置下进行40K次页面访问的测量研究；3）开发自定义检测工具识别网站对自动化的探测行为，建立机器人检测技术分类体系；4）开展头部欺骗实验，分离头部信号与JavaScript环境探测的影响。

Result: 1）83%的论文未讨论机器人检测拦截；2）Chromium headless软拦截率15%，其他配置7%；3）82%的拦截归因于机器人检测（59%供应商确认，23%条件依赖推断），Cloudflare拦截率37%，Akamai 26%；4）75%的Chromium-headless-only拦截由头部信号引起；5）JavaScript环境探测比当前拦截率显示的更广泛。

Conclusion: 机器人检测造成了系统性且与供应商相关的样本损失，但网络测量社区既未测量也未报告这一问题；对具体测量结果的下游影响有待未来研究。

Abstract: Browser automation frameworks are essential tools for security and privacy research on the web, yet bot detection scripts increasingly probe their artifacts, threatening measurement validity as automated browsers may be blocked or served different content. Prior work measures detection deployment, while we measure blocking-induced sample loss. Through a literature survey of top-tier security, privacy, and web measurement venues, we find that 83% of papers omit any discussion of bot detection blocking. To address this gap, we conduct a measurement study of 10,000 websites across four browser configurations (40K page visits in total) to quantify detection prevalence and employed techniques. Using custom instrumentation to detect when sites probe for automation, we develop a taxonomy of bot detection techniques and measure how often they appear in practice. Chromium headless encounters a 15% soft block rate compared to 7% for other configurations. Across all conditions, 82% of blocks are attributable to bot detection (59% vendor-confirmed, 23% inferred from condition-dependent blocking), predominantly by providers with integrated bot detection such as Cloudflare (37% block rate) and Akamai (26%). A header spoofing experiment establishes that 75% of Chromium-headless-only blocks are caused by header-level signals alone, yet JavaScript-based environment probing is more extensive than current blocking rates suggest. These findings demonstrate that bot detection creates systematic, provider-correlated sample loss that the web measurement community neither measures nor reports. The downstream effect on specific measurement outcomes remains future work.

</details>


### [136] [When Good Verifiers Go Bad: Self-Improving VLMs Can Regress on New Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14629)
*Jianzhe Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，在视觉语言模型的自我改进中，验证器驱动的自DPO方法存在一个反直觉的失败模式：当验证器在目标任务上的准确率低于某个阈值时，更强的验证器反而会导致学生模型性能更严重的退化（比随机验证器还差），而DPO训练损失仍在下降。作者给出了基于方差定理的机制解释，并提出了部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的验证器驱动自DPO方法假设更强的验证器能带来更强的学生模型（单调性假设），但本文发现这一假设在验证器质量具有高度任务特异性的情况下可能失效，需要揭示这一反直觉现象的机制和条件。

Method: 在MathVista、MMMU和BLINK三个基准上构建四阶开源验证器阶梯，使用Qwen-3-VL-2B和Qwen-2.5-VL-3B两个学生模型，测量不同验证器在不同任务上的rubric准确率，并与DPO训练后的学生性能进行对比分析。

Result: 验证器在MMMU上的任务rubric准确率仅8%-23%（低于阈值），所有验证器均导致学生模型性能下降3.4-10.9个百分点；且出现置信度反转：更准确但仍低于阈值的验证器反而比近乎随机的验证器造成更大的性能退化。

Conclusion: 部署前应测量目标任务rubric准确率，按目标任务质量而非参数量排序验证器，并在高于阈值时将对验证器的投入视为计算预算上限。

Abstract: Verifier-driven self-DPO is a common recipe for self-improving production visual-language models. In this setup, a frozen verifier scores candidate generations, the top- and bottom-scoring candidates form a preference example, and DPO updates the learner. The deployment-time assumption is monotone: a stronger verifier should yield a stronger student. We show that this assumption can fail because verifier quality is highly task-specific. On a four-rung open-source verifier ladder across MathVista, MMMU, and BLINK, the same verifiers that are above-threshold and improve a Qwen-3-VL-2B student on MathVista become sub-threshold on MMMU, where their task-rubric accuracy drops to 8% to 23%.
  In this regime, every verifier we tested silently regresses the student, producing drops of 3.4 to 10.9 percentage points below the frozen baseline while the DPO training loss continues to decrease. The regression replicates on a second student, Qwen-2.5-VL-3B. Moreover, within the failure regime, damage is confidence-inverted: the more accurate-but-still-wrong verifier causes larger regression than a near-random verifier, suggesting that progress-gated replay amplifies confidently wrong preference pairs. We give a compact mechanistic explanation via a variance theorem for progress-gated replay and its direction-mismatch failure mode.
  The deployment message is operational rather than purely diagnostic: before running any verifier-driven loop, teams should measure target-task rubric accuracy, rank verifiers by target-task rubric quality rather than parameter count, and treat diminishing returns in above-threshold regimes as a verifier-side compute budget cap.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [LoMC: Localized Multidirectional Correction for Refusal Suppression in Routed Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13709)
*Yan Hong,Kedong Xiu,Wei Li,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出局部多方向校正（LoMC）框架，通过支持门控干预机制在路由MoE和混合MoE基础模型中抑制拒绝行为，在紧凑干预范围内提高非拒绝目标响应行为并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义方向编辑会干扰通用计算，而仅支持专家的编辑缺乏足够容量来纠正异构拒绝表示，需要一种既能增加校正容量又不扩大干预范围的方法。

Method: LoMC采用支持-校正执行顺序：先识别紧凑编辑支持，再将原型校正方向聚合为逐层校正方向，最后仅在选定的支持内应用秩一逐层校正，利用编辑支持作为结构门控约束来增加校正容量。

Result: 在四个路由骨干网络的纯文本和多模态安全基准测试中，LoMC显著提高了非拒绝目标响应行为，同时在紧凑干预范围内保持了通用能力。

Conclusion: LoMC通过支持门控干预框架有效解决了路由MoE和混合MoE模型中拒绝抑制校正容量与干预范围之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: We study controlled post-training refusal suppression in routed MoE and hybrid-MoE foundation models, aiming to increase non-refusal target-response behavior while preserving general capability under a compact intervention footprint. Existing broad direction-based edits can perturb general-purpose computation, whereas support-only expert edits often lack sufficient capacity to correct heterogeneous refusal representations. To address this limitation, we introduce Localized Multidirectional Correction (LoMC), a support-gated intervention framework that follows a support-then-correction execution order: it first identifies a compact edit support, then aggregates prototype correction directions into layer-wise correction directions, and finally applies rank-one layer-wise correction only within the selected support. By using the edit support as a structural gating constraint, LoMC increases correction capacity without expanding the intervention scope. Experiments on text-only and multimodal safety benchmarks across four routed backbones show that LoMC substantially improves non-refusal target-response behavior while maintaining general capability under a compact intervention footprint.

</details>


### [138] [Recursively Trained Diffusion Models: Limiting Collapse Distribution and Spectral Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13796)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成模型递归训练中的模型坍缩现象，证明即使完美学习也会因早期停止导致分布偏移，且递归收敛到唯一的极限分布。该极限分布可解析表达为数据分布的高斯平滑混合，递归训练相当于一个低通滤波器，高阶模态被强烈衰减。提出退火截断策略可消除递归复合效应，并证明该结论在离散化和分数估计误差下具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解递归训练中模型坍缩的极限分布及其收敛速率，填补现有理论中"收敛到何种分布"和"收敛多快"两个空白。

Method: 理论分析：证明递归训练收敛到唯一极限分布，该分布为数据分布的无限混合高斯平滑。利用Hermite谱分解揭示低通滤波机制。提出退火截断策略并证明其渐近消除递归复合效应。分析离散化和分数估计误差下的鲁棒性保证。

Result: 递归训练几何收敛到唯一极限分布；高阶模态被强烈衰减；退火截断策略可消除递归复合；理论结果在误差下具有Wasserstein-2球内的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 递归训练中的模型坍缩主要由反向扩散的早期停止驱动，而非学习不完美；通过退火截断策略可有效缓解。

Abstract: Recursive training of generative models on their own outputs can lead to model collapse, a compounding drift away from the true data distribution. Existing theoretical works bound finite-round error accumulation in the context of diffusion models, but two questions remain open:~what distribution does the recursion converge to, and how fast? We answer both, isolating a mechanism distinct from imperfect learning: even with perfect score estimation and exact sampling, the early stopping of the reverse diffusion (required for numerical stability) drives a progressive drift away from the data distribution. We prove that this recursion converges geometrically to a unique limiting distribution, which admits a closed-form characterization as an infinite mixture of increasingly Gaussian-smoothed versions of the data distribution. A Hermite spectral decomposition of this limit reveals that recursive training acts as a low-pass filter: higher-order modes, which encode fine non-Gaussian structure, are attenuated much more strongly than coarse modes. This spectral picture motivates annealed truncation schedules that progressively shrink truncation times across retraining rounds; we prove that any schedule converging to $0$ asymptotically eliminates recursive compounding. Finally, we show our idealized characterization is robust: in the presence of discretization and score estimation errors, the learned distribution remains in a Wasserstein-2 ball around the ideal limit, with mode-dependent contraction rates that contract high-order errors faster than low-order ones. We validate the theory on synthetic Gaussian mixtures and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [139] [Adaptive Nucleus Truncation for Long-Form Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13982)
*Ousmane Amadou Dia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应核截断采样（ANTS），将固定阈值的top-nσ采样扩展为自适应rollout控制机制，通过熵条件控制器动态调整截断宽度，并在截断不安全时保留无截断回退臂。在33B总参/4B活跃参数的稀疏MoE推理模型上，ANTS在8K、16K和32K生成预算下分别平均提升1.9、3.8和5.2个百分点，尤其在指令遵循和数学推理任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截断采样方法（如top-p、min-p、固定top-nσ）依赖固定阈值，无法适应熵值、任务难度、训练阶段或生成预算的变化，限制了长序列推理的稳定性和性能。

Method: ANTS选择温度缩放前最大logit周围的标准化邻域，使用熵条件控制器自适应调整截断宽度，并设计无截断回退臂以在截断不安全时稳定训练。

Result: 在33B总参/4B活跃参数的稀疏MoE推理模型上，ANTS在8K/16K/32K预算下分别提升1.9/3.8/5.2个百分点；IFBench在32K提升超10点，AIME 2025提升7点；Codeforces在8K落后基线但16K和32K大幅逆转并提升ELO。

Conclusion: 采样器设计不应仅被视为解码超参数，而应作为稳定和扩展长预算推理系统的一部分。

Abstract: Sampling plays an important role in long-form language-model reasoning. Over thousands of decoding steps, small changes in the candidate token set can compound into different reasoning trajectories, stability profiles, and final answers. Existing truncation methods such as top-$p$, min-$p$, and fixed top-$nσ$ sampling improve over unrestricted sampling, but they rely on fixed thresholds that cannot adapt to changes in entropy, task difficulty, training stage, or generation budget. We introduce Adaptive Nucleus Truncation Sampling (ANTS), which extends top-\(nσ\) sampling from a fixed decoding rule into an adaptive rollout-control mechanism for long-form generation. ANTS selects standardized neighborhoods around the maximum logit before temperature scaling, adapts the truncation width using an entropy-conditioned controller, and retains a no-truncation fallback arm to stabilize training when truncation becomes unsafe. On a 33B-total / 4B-active sparse Mixture-of-Experts reasoning model, ANTS improves average performance over percentage-based benchmarks by +1.9, +3.8, and +5.2 points at 8K, 16K, and 32K generation budgets, respectively. The strongest gains appear on instruction following and mathematical reasoning, with IFBench improving by more than 10 points at 32K and AIME 2025 improving by 7 points. Code generation reveals an important budget interaction. On Codeforces, ANTS trails the baseline at 8K, but reverses this gap and substantially improves ELO at 16K and 32K. These results suggest that sampler design should be treated not just as a decoding hyperparameter, but as part of how we stabilize and scale long-budget reasoning.

</details>


### [140] [A General Framework for Decision Trees via Bregman Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13984)
*Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bregman散度的CART决策树泛化框架，将传统的分裂准则统一为凸几何原理，并研究了生成凸函数的强凸性、光滑性等性质对杂质增益、估计量稳定性和一致性的理论影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CART算法虽然广泛使用，但其杂质准则通常是针对特定模型以ad hoc方式引入的，缺乏统一的几何和凸分析基础。Bregman散度家族提供了丰富的损失函数（如KL散度、泊松散度、Itakura-Saito散度等），且具有深厚的几何结构，因此作者希望将CART框架推广至Bregman散度，以统一推导和解释不同模型下的分裂准则。

Method: 以Bregman散度替代传统CART中的平方误差损失，作为节点分裂的杂质度量；利用生成凸函数的几何性质（强凸性、光滑性等）统一推导分裂准则；在算法层面构建广义决策树，并在理论上分析杂质增益、估计量的稳定性和一致性。

Result: 得到了一个统一的Bregman-CART框架，能够涵盖多种统计模型下的决策树；理论分析表明生成凸函数的强凸性和光滑性直接影响父-子节点间的杂质增益以及估计量的稳定性和一致性。

Conclusion: Bregman散度提供了一种统一的、基于凸几何原理的决策树构建方法，不仅拓展了CART的适用范围，还揭示了分裂准则背后的几何本质，为不同统计模型下的决策树提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Decision trees are one of the fundamental tools in statistical learning due to their interpretability, flexibility, and their ability to adapt to nonlinear structures. Among them, the Classification and Regression Trees, introduced by Breiman, Friedman, Olshen, and Stone in 1984, became one of the most influential algorithms and remains one of the most widely used methods for classification and regression problems. On the other hand, Bregman divergences, introduced by Lev Bregman in 1967 in the context of convex optimization, provide a broad family of loss functions that naturally generalize the squared Euclidean distance. This family includes, among others, the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the Poisson divergence, and the Itakura-Saito divergence, as well as several losses associated with distributions belonging to the exponential family. Moreover, Bregman divergences possess a rich geometric structure and deep connections with convex analysis and information geometry. In this work, we propose a generalization of the CART paradigm based on Bregman divergences, thereby obtaining a broader family of decision trees adapted to different statistical models and underlying geometries. Although algorithms such as CART or classical implementations such as rpart incorporate different impurity criteria, these are usually introduced in an ad hoc manner for each specific model. In contrast, the Bregman divergence approach provides a unified framework that allows these criteria to be derived and interpreted from common convex and geometric principles. Beyond the algorithmic construction, we also investigate theoretical properties of these trees. In particular, we study how properties of the generating convex function -- such as strong convexity or smoothness -- influence impurity gains between parent and child nodes, as well as stability and consistency properties of the estimator.

</details>


### [141] [Geometric Domain Adaptation via Optimal Transport for Linear Regression in R^2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14023)
*Brian Britos,Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在二维空间中，当源域与目标域之间存在旋转、平移或位似变换时，使用p-范数代价（p≥2）的最优传输可以恢复底层几何映射。基于此理论，结合K-means与最优传输提出了一种线性回归域自适应方法，在目标数据稀缺时表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最优传输在监督域自适应中恢复二维几何变换（旋转、平移、位似）的理论保证，并利用该洞察设计可解释的线性回归自适应方法，避免依赖黑盒深度学习。

Method: 理论分析：证明在ℝ²中使用p-范数（p≥2）的最优传输映射可精确恢复旋转、平移和位似变换。实践方法：结合K-means聚类与最优传输估计底层映射，进而实现对线性回归模型的域自适应。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提方法在目标数据稀缺场景下优于基线方法，且保持了模型的解释性和理论可理解性。

Conclusion: 最优传输能在特定几何变换下恢复映射关系，为经典机器学习模型提供了一种兼具理论深度与实践价值的域自适应方案。

Abstract: Optimal Transport has become recently a powerful method for domain adaptation by aligning source and target distributions. We study a supervised domain adaptation problem where source and target domains are related by a rotation or a translation or a homothety in $\mathbb{R}^2$. We prove that the optimal transport map recovers the underlying map when using a $p-$norm cost with $p \geq 2$. Based on this insight, we develop a method combining $K-$means and optimal transport to estimate the underlying map, enabling adaptation of linear regression models when target data is scarce. Simulations demonstrate improved performance over baseline methods. Rather than relying on highly expressive deep learning architectures, we focus on classical machine learning models to emphasize interpretability and theoretical insight. This perspective allows us to explicitly characterize the role of optimal transport in recovering geometric transformations such as rotations, translations, and homotheties.
  Our contributions include a theoretical result linking optimal transport and rotations, translations and homothecies in $\mathbb{R}^2$, and a practical method for adaptation in linear regression offering both conceptual clarity and applied value in domain adaptation tasks in this space.

</details>


### [142] [Anytime-Valid Confirmation of Label-Shift Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14028)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 当标注目标样本稀缺时，本文提出利用预定义的标签偏移校正假设，通过条件e-值构建非负鞅，将常规模型监控转化为形式化的时序检验，用于验证标注数据是否支持假设的偏移校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小型科学部署中，标注目标结果可能过于稀少，无法进行可靠的偏移估计。现有方法依赖未标注目标输入，但无法充分利用先验领域知识中预定义的标签偏移校正假设。

Method: 证明预定义标签偏移校正预测与源预测之间每观测的似然比是一个条件e-值，其累积乘积构成非负鞅；利用Ville不等式提供任意有效（anytime-valid）的确认规则；对数鞅等于累积负对数预测密度（NLPD）差距，将常规模型监控转化为形式化时序检验；对高斯过程源提供闭合解。

Result: 在高斯过程回归仿真中验证了：类型I错误控制、有限样本功效、校准敏感性以及小批量场景下基于可靠先验相较于标签重估计的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法在标签样本稀缺时，通过预定义偏移假设的时序检验提供了比直接重估计更可靠的确认机制，但需注意拒绝仅支持假设成立而非精确估计偏移程度。

Abstract: In small-batch scientific deployments, labeled target outcomes may be too scarce for reliable shift estimation even when unlabeled target inputs are available. We address the complementary setting where the practitioner has a pre-specified label-shift correction from domain knowledge and asks whether incoming labeled outcomes support it.
